MagicGlass P0 on-device probe findings
======================================
Date       : 2026-11-11 22:20 - 22:38 GMT+8
Device     : HONOR AAK-AN00 (HNAAK), Android 17, API 37
Build      : AAK-AN00 11.0.0.117(SP3C00E117R102P2)
Connection : adb USB, uid=2000(shell), su available

READ-ONLY COMPLIANCE
--------------------
- No reboot, no soft reboot, no zygote restart, no adb root/remount.
- Zero writes to the phone filesystem. `uiautomator dump /dev/stdout` streams
  the XML to the PC; framework/APK bytes are streamed with `adb exec-out cat`
  into local files. No /sdcard temp file, no rm, no settings put, no push.
- Only non-file device state changes: three `am start -W` calls to open
  Settings pages for view-tree sampling.
- All artifacts live on the PC under magicglass/probe/probe-report/.

1. THE BIGGEST CORRECTION: the documented SDK classes do NOT exist in the ROM
---------------------------------------------------------------------------
DEX-level class_def parsing of framework.jar, framework-magic.jar and all nine
scoped APKs found NO class named
    com.hihonor.uikit.transparency.api.HnVisualEffectLayout
    com.hihonor.uikit.transparency.api.HnGlassEffect
    HnMaterialType / HnSceneType / HnWidgetSize (in that package)
Those are developer-SDK aar classes. A normal LSPosed module running inside
com.android.settings cannot see them unless it embeds the aar itself.
=> The previous MaterialBridge was targeting a non-existent API. Rewritten.

4. Real Settings view tree (from dumpsys, not uiautomator)
---------------------------------------------------------
uiautomator fails on Settings pages:
  L1 "ERROR: null root node returned by UiTestAutomationBridge."
  L2 "ERROR: could not get idle state."
Cause: ViewRootHandler message queue contains
  com.honor.hb.HonorRenderer$9 / HonorRenderer$11
i.e. the already-installed HonorBG bridge is posting pre-draw callbacks every
frame, so the window never reaches idle. dumpsys activity top works around it.

L1 (com.android.settings/.HWSettings) hierarchy:
  DecorView 0,0-1272,2800
    ImageView 0,0-1272,2800                <- injected by the existing module
    ... HnTransparentPattern (app:id/content_parent)
      HnGradientBlurTopContainer / BottomContainer
      content_frame -> main_content_scrollable_container
        HwCoordinatorLayout -> HwAppBarLayout / HwCollapsingToolbarLayout
        content_layout -> dashboard_root
          HwRecyclerView #app:id/dashboard_container
            rows #app:id/dashboard_tile, each 182 px tall:
              0,628-810 / 810-992 / 992-1174 / 1174-1356 / 1356-1538
              gap ~63 px between groups (1538 -> 1601)
              next group 1601-1783 ... 2329-2511, gap 63, 2574-2938
            row classes: LinearLayout, SummaryPreferenceLayout,
                         HwImageView, HwTextView, HnGradientBlur...
  Also present: StylableLayout, AppbarBlurBg, TransActionMenuView,
  ToolbarImageView, HwToolbar, HwScrollbarView.
Resolution 1272x2800 (same geometry family as the WKL-AN20 constants in the
HonorBG bridge, but thresholds must still be re-measured per P0 decision).

L2 (Settings$WifiSettingsActivity): 1 dashboard_tile row in dump.
L3-like (applications.InstalledAppDetailsTop): 0 dashboard_tile rows,
  4 view-hierarchy blocks, transparency config appears 16 times.
=> Note: these L2/L3 samples were opened via intent actions, not by tapping
   through the real navigation chain. A true L3 page still needs a manual run.

5. Existing module interference / recommendation
------------------------------------------------
com.ciallo.hyperbackground is installed and ACTIVE in the Settings process
(HonorRenderer callbacks, full-screen ImageView under DecorView). It pollutes
the native view tree and will collide with a second module that also hooks
backgrounds / ItemDecorations.
Recommendation: do NOT uninstall the APK (that deletes phone files). Instead
temporarily disable its scope in LSPosed, then `am force-stop
com.android.settings` (no reboot, no file writes, fully reversible). Do this
before installing the new module for P1. If LSPosed requires a soft reboot to
apply, ask the user first, then use `setprop ctl.restart zygote` only.

8.1 CORRECTION of section 4: uiautomator failure has TWO causes
---------------------------------------------------------------
On the REAL L3 page (移动网络 -> 个人热点 -> 更多共享设置,
Settings$TetherSettingsActivity) uiautomator STILL returns
  "ERROR: null root node returned by UiTestAutomationBridge"
even with the old module disabled and 0 HonorRenderer callbacks.
The native page itself keeps the window from reaching idle:
  HwProgressBar (list header), HnGradientBlurTopContainer (0,0-1272,394),
  HnGradientBlurBottomContainer (0,2737-1272,2800), HnTransparentPattern,
  HnSelectionActionView#follow_menu. Only callback left is
  callback=com.hihonor.hianalytics.threads.b (system analytics).
=> Runtime card detection must NOT use uiautomator. Use the in-process View
   tree from a pre-draw callback (the module already hooks Activity lifecycle).
   dumpsys View Hierarchy remains the offline ground truth.

8.3 Clean geometry (uiautomator L1, dumpsys L3)
-----------------------------------------------
L1 .HWSettings / fragment DashboardSummary
  HwRecyclerView #app:id/dashboard_container -> LinearLayout #dashboard_tile
  11 clickable rows, width 1272:
    [416,742] h=326  -> STANDALONE CARD
    [805,1715] 5 x 182px -> GROUP CARD
    [1778,2688] 5 x 182px -> GROUP CARD
  group gap = 63px.
L3 .Settings$TetherSettingsActivity / fragment NetShareTetherSettings
  SettingsRecyclerView #app:id/list -> com.android.settings.MinHeightLinearLayout
  rows: [373,541] [604,772] [835,1003] 168px,
        [1066,1293] 227px (switch + summary),
        [1356,1524] 168px, [1587,1880] 293px, [1880,2107] 227px
  gap = 63px; an explicit 63px spacer LinearLayout [1524,1587] between groups.
=> Row height is VARIABLE (168 / 227 / 293). NEVER hard-code it. The only
   reliable group separator is the 63px gap; 30px is a safe cut-off.
=> L1 and L3 use DIFFERENT RecyclerView and row classes: match on geometry +
   "direct child of a *RecyclerView", not on ROM names or resource ids.

8.4 CORRECTION of PageLevel: the level signal is the ACTIVITY STACK
--------------------------------------------------------------------
dumpsys shows every SubSettings Activity hosts EXACTLY ONE fragment with
mBackStackNesting=0 (DashboardSummary on L1, NetShareTetherSettings on L3).
Fragment back-stack depth is therefore ALWAYS 0 and the old detector would
classify every page as L1. Real chain in task #996 (4 activities):
  Hist #0 com.android.settings/.HWSettings                    rootOfTask=true -> L1
  Hist #1 com.android.settings/.Settings$WirelessSettingsActivity             -> L2
  Hist #2 com.android.settings/com.hihonor.settingslib.SubSettings           -> L3
  Hist #3 com.android.settings/.Settings$TetherSettingsActivity              -> L3+
=> New detector tracks a per-task Activity stack in-process (deduplicated
   onCreate/onResume push, prune on finishing/destroyed), index maps to level.
   Verified class rules, AOSP intent extras, top-blur container and hosted
   fragment name are fallbacks only.

never hard-code a constant name.

NOTE: section 1 remains correct but was too narrow. Only the `api.` package
(HnVisualEffectLayout / HnGlassEffect / HnMaterialType) is absent. The
INTERNAL uikit implementation is present and is the correct A-route target.

8.6 FINAL CHOICE (user asked the assistant to decide, 2026-11-11)
------------------------------------------------------------------
PRIMARY  = HOST_UIKIT. In the host process, instantiate one
           com.hihonor.uikit.transparency.widget.HnTransFrameLayout per card
           block inside a decor overlay; align with pre-draw; clear the stock
           white row backgrounds; let onConfigurationChanged() follow the
           system transparency slider / liquid-vs-frosted switch / dark mode.
  Why: present in ALL nine scopes, no hidden-API exemption needed, uses the
       system's own material repositories (most literal "system automatic"),
       naturally gives one continuous group card per block, no ViewHolder
       reparenting, and Backdrop/LiquidTab refraction is already implemented
       by the ROM.
SECONDARY = APP_CONTAINER (same family, for scopes missing StyleType/SceneType)
            and BOOT_BACKDROP (View.setBackdropRenderEffect + LiquidGlassMaterial).
FALLBACK  = Backdrop/LiquidTab AGSL refraction, only if both above fail.
NEVER     = MIUI setMi* APIs; developer-SDK aar classes (absent from ROM).
This also REMOVES the previous hard blocker "must install :probe to resolve
hidden-API access": the primary path needs no hidden API. Runtime probe is now
only needed to map obfuscated StyleType constants and confirm
getRemotePreferences("magicglass") writability.

API references, zero reboot/phone-write commands in scripts.

9. P1 IMPLEMENTATION (2026-11-11, continued)
============================================
Scope of this round: complete the runtime rendering loop and the per-level
configuration UI. No device commands were issued; both user restrictions
(no reboot, no writes to phone files) stayed in force.

9.1 New / rewritten files
-------------------------
  NEW RenderController.java   the core P1 piece. One instance per Activity:
      - decor overlay (index 0) -> backdrop at 0/1/2, glass host at 3;
      - ViewTreeObserver.OnPreDrawListener drives geometry sync while the
        RecyclerView scrolls (uiautomator cannot be used: proven non-idle);
      - 32ms throttle + FNV rect signature + cached RecyclerView refs;
      - pooled HnTransFrameLayout containers, rebuilt only when the glass
        parameter fingerprint changes (material uniforms are snapshots);
      - strips stock white backgrounds from each row AND up to 4 ancestors,
        re-clears them when RecyclerView rebinds, restores originals on
        detach/disable; chrome / HnTransparentPattern are protected;
      - config-changed broadcast receiver with debounce;
      - BOOT_BACKDROP fallback applies a blur RenderEffect to rows in place.
  NEW BackdropLayer.java      per-level image / random pool / video:
      - TextureView + MediaPlayer (NOT SurfaceView: it breaks RenderEffect);
      - two-pass sampled Bitmap decode via content://, alpha, scaleType,
        View.setRenderEffect blur, tint/dim overlay, timed pool rotation;
      - every GPU path guarded, failure leaves the ROM backdrop intact.
  NEW ConfigBus.java          host-side reader: own ContentProvider first
                              (freshest), LSPosed remote prefs as fallback.
  NEW ConfigStore.java        module-side writer + explicit broadcast to all
                              9 scoped packages + SAF media import.
  NEW ModuleProvider.java     read-only content://com.magicglass.provider,
                              /config -> MatrixCursor, /media/* -> FD with
                              path-traversal validation.
  NEW SettingsActivity.java   pure-Java UI: slot spinner, every parameter,
                              per-parameter "inherit from global" switch,
                              image/video/pool pickers, save/reset/diagnostics.
  NEW probe/compile_check.py  generates a minimal android.* / libxposed API
                              stub set and runs a REAL javac compile of all
                              13 Java sources (catches undefined symbols).
  REWRITTEN GlassEntry.java   hooks -> per-Activity RenderController, remote
                              prefs handed to ConfigBus, controller pruning.
  CardScanner.java            stateful Scanner with cached list refs,
                              GROUP/STANDALONE per-level filters, overlay-local
                              coordinates using getLocationOnScreen().
  ConfigContract.java         Source abstraction (SharedPreferences OR Map),
                              glassFingerprint()/bgFingerprint(), 2 new bg keys.
  MaterialBridge.java         FIXED a real compile error: createLayout()
                              referenced an undefined `glassEffect` variable;
                              signature is now (Context, Snapshot, Object).
  AndroidManifest.xml         declares SettingsActivity + ModuleProvider.
  module.prop / build.gradle  version bumped to 0.2.0-p1.

9.2 Why the config channel is a provider
----------------------------------------
MODE_WORLD_READABLE is gone and a hooked system process cannot read
/data/data/com.magicglass. The module writes its own SharedPreferences and
exposes them through an exported read-only provider; host processes query it
on config change (1.5s cache) and fall back to getRemotePreferences. Both
channels feed the same ConfigContract.Source String map. No system partition,
Settings.Global key, or device file is touched by the probe tooling.

--------------------------------------
  bash -n probe/adb_probe.sh                      OK
  py_compile scan_jars.py / dex_api.py / compile_check.py   OK
  XML parse of both AndroidManifest.xml          OK
  javalang parse of all 13 Java files            OK
  javac against 61 generated API stubs           COMPILE_OK (128 classes)
  zero MIUI setMi* / com.ciallo references in sources       OK (comments only)
  zero reboot / phone-write commands in adb_probe.sh        OK
  scope.list = 9 entries, java_init.list points to GlassEntry / ProbeEntry.

  NOTE: javac stubs are NOT a substitute for a real Gradle/AGP build. They
  validate symbols and signatures only; resource packaging of
  META-INF/xposed/* must still be checked with `unzip -l *.apk | grep xposed`.

9.4 What to do next (on the device)
-----------------------------------
  1. Put libxposed api-101.jar into app/libs/api-101.jar.
  2. Open magicglass/ in Android Studio (JDK 21, SDK 35), build :app.
  3. Install the APK, tick all 9 scopes in LSPosed, keep the OLD HonorBG
     module disabled. A soft reboot (`setprop ctl.restart zygote`) needs the
     user's explicit approval; no agent command will issue it.
  4. Open MagicGlass from the launcher: set GLASS_ENABLED on GLOBAL, then
     override L1/L2/L3 or EXT_* slots; pick an image/video for one level.
  5. Open Settings L1 -> L2 -> L3 and watch the MagicGlass log:
        SDK usable=true mode=HOST_UIKIT
        page level=L1/L2/L3 ... taskIndex=...
        controller: container construction failed ... (if any)
  6. Optional: install :probe, read MagicGlassProbe log for the obfuscated
     StyleType/SceneType constant names and hidden-API availability.

9.5 Residual risks (items 1 and 2 CLOSED by section 10)
-------------------------------------------------------
  - [CLOSED] Empty HnTransFrameLayout does dispatch-draw; see 10.3.
  - [CLOSED] StyleType / SceneType names are NOT obfuscated; see 10.1/10.2.
  - systemmanager / wallet ship HnTransFrameLayout but no StyleType strings;
    APP_CONTAINER mode there needs P3 verification.
  - Clearing ancestor backgrounds is the most invasive part. If a page shows
    a wrong background, narrow MAX_ANCESTOR_DEPTH or extend keepBackground().
  - Official guidance discourages transparency on list cards: one container
    per block + setBlurType/skipRenderLayer is the planned mitigation.
  - Video + RenderEffect interaction on TextureView must be measured for
    frame rate and battery on a real device.

10. OFFLINE DISASSEMBLY OF THE HOST UIKIT (2026-11-11 23:35, zero device cmds)
================================================================================
Tool: probe/dex_deep.py (androguard 4.1.4), input = offline/com.android.settings.apk
Full dump: probe/probe-report/deep_uikit.txt (5070 lines).
NOTE: androguard cannot parse framework.jar from this Android 17 build
("4 is not a valid HiddenApiClassDataItem.DomapiApiFlag"); dex_api.py still
gives its signatures. Numeric GlassParam ranges remain an open item.

10.1 StyleType - FIELD names obfuscated, Enum.name() PRESERVED
--------------------------------------------------------------
Lcom/hihonor/uikit/transparency/style/StyleType; <clinit> literals:
  field a -> "NORMAL"                    ordinal 0, mode 0
  field b -> "TRANSPARENCY"              ordinal 1, mode 1
  field c -> "FROSTED_GLASS_TRANSPARENCY" ordinal 2, mode 2
  field d -> "LIQUID_GLASS_TRANSPARENT"  ordinal 3, mode 4
  private final int mode  (unobfuscated field name)
DECISIVE: Lwo/f;->e(StyleType)Z returns (style != null && style != NORMAL).
L.../widget/a;->N(...) returns immediately when that check fails, so no
AbsViewStyle is ever built. Therefore "follow" MUST NOT leave the container
at its default -- that default IS NORMAL, i.e. NO GLASS AT ALL. The old
applyHostStyle() returned early on "follow", which was the single biggest
P1 defect. Correct mapping:
  follow  -> TRANSPARENCY              (system picks liquid/frosted + grade)
  liquid  -> LIQUID_GLASS_TRANSPARENT
  frosted -> FROSTED_GLASS_TRANSPARENCY

10.2 SceneType - names preserved, mode is a BIT MASK
----------------------------------------------------
  TransparencyOn 1, TransparencyOFF 2, Dark 4, Light 8, Immersive 16,
  General 32, GradientBlur 64, UniformBlur 128, BigWidget 256,
  SmallWidget 512, FollowColorPick 1024, FollowUIMODE 2048,
  ForceColorMode 4096, NoBlur 8192, MATTE 16384.
  public int mode; SceneType.c() returns it. SceneType.k(I)/t(I)/p(I) are
  bit tests. HnTrans*Layout defaults to SceneType->l == "SmallWidget".
HnTransEffectDelegate.<init>(Context, AttributeSet, View) reads the whole
style config from obtainStyledAttributes; when AttributeSet == null (which
is exactly what `new HnTransFrameLayout(Context)` does) that entire block
is skipped and the delegate falls through with style=NORMAL, scene mask=0.
=> We MUST call setViewStyleWithScenes(StyleType, int sceneMask, int, Bundle)
explicitly. Recommended mask for a large grouped card:
  General(32) | BigWidget(256) | FollowUIMODE(2048) = 2336
and for the immersive option Immersive(16) | BigWidget(256) | FollowUIMODE(2048).
Host code itself uses SceneType.General.getMode() for its dialogs.

10.3 Empty container DOES dispatch-draw (no placeholder child needed)
----------------------------------------------------------------------
HnTransFrameLayout <init>(Context) -> ... <init>(Context,AttributeSet,I,I):
  setWillNotDraw(false)
  scene = widget/a.r(Context, attrs)   // attrs == null -> SmallWidget
  delegate = new widget/a(Context, attrs, this)
Its dispatchDraw(Canvas) is unconditional:
  delegate.i(canvas); super.dispatchDraw(canvas)
i() -> g(canvas, delegate.z). g() reads AbsViewStyle.getSharedArgs(), the
view's getWidth()/getHeight(), radius and Path, then calls Lwo/b;->a(...).
j()/t() also require width>0 && height>0 before initEffects(). So an EMPTY
container with a non-zero LayoutParams and an injected style WILL paint;
no transparent placeholder child is required (and adding one is harmless).
onConfigurationChanged(Configuration) forwards to AbsViewStyle only when it
is non-null -- another reason a style MUST be injected for "system automatic".

10.4 setCornerRadius(F) side effect
------------------------------------
setCornerRadius(F) -> delegate.I(F) -> J(F, false), which sets delegate.z =
false and triggers B() -> j() -> t(); t() recomputes z from whether the
style's sharedArgs carry a Drawable. So repeated setCornerRadius calls from
the per-frame configure() are wasteful and can transiently suppress drawing.
Fix: set the radius ONCE at container creation (from GEO_RADIUS_DP x density)
and leave configure() to only update LayoutParams.

10.5 Other verified internals
------------------------------
  getMediator() returns Luo/a; widget/a.h(...) builds the AbsViewStyle via
  Luo/a.c(StyleType,I,I,Bundle) or .d(StyleType,I,I) -- that is where the
  liquid/frosted x grade x widget-size material repos are selected.
  57 data/repo/* classes exist (high/mid/low grade x liquid/gradient x
  large/small widget x general/immersive); their <clinit> only holds color
  strings ("#4d000000", "#4dFFFFFF"), numeric uniforms come from the
  framework material layer, not from these DEX constants.
  HnTransLinearLayout.<clinit> references ViewEffectType c/d/e; the stack is
  a real effect system, not a simple blur drawable.

10.6 Code changes made from this section
----------------------------------------
  MaterialBridge: enum resolution by Enum.name() (not toString()/contains),
  mode-bit scene mask, explicit style injection for ALL material values,
  4-arg setViewStyleWithScenes with 2-arg fallback, radius set once,
  describeStyleTypes()/describeSceneTypes() now print real names + modes.
  ConfigContract: documented real enum values, GLASS_SCENE options.
  RenderController: unchanged (its configure() is geometry-signature gated).

  Environment (all read-only): LSPosed 2.2.1 (7912), framework API 102,
  Zygisk variant (zygisk_lsposed), MagicOS / Android 17 (API 37),

  12.1 Module state -- CONFIRMED GOOD
    - com.magicglass 0.2.1-p1 (code 3) installed, enabled=true, not suspended.
    - LSPosed module page: "启用模块" switch ON; scope list shows
      com.android.settings, com.android.systemui, com.hihonor.id,
      com.hihonor.wallet, com.hihonor.controlcenter,

  12.2 Observation
    - L1 screenshot (HWSettings): plain light stock cards, no liquid glass,
      no custom background, no rendering artefacts.
    - `adb logcat -d` contains ZERO lines with tag MagicGlass. IMPORTANT:
      LSPosed 2.2.1 does NOT forward XposedModule.log() to logcat for this
      module; module logs live in the manager's 日志 -> 模块日志 page.
      (Another module, HonorDuoBar, does appear as tag LSPosedFramework.)
      Consequence: logcat alone cannot be used to judge whether the module
      loaded; use the manager log page.
    - Manager log page, search "magic": MagicGlass entries at 23:53:14 -
      23:53:26 in pid 8637 (com.android.settings), 8788 (systemui), 12432
      (id), 13401 (assistant), 11418 (parentcontrol) etc, ALL of them:
          "render hooks installed for <pkg>"
          "hooked callActivityOnCreate" / "hooked callActivityOnResume"
          "starting-window suppression scheduled (P2)" (systemui)
    - NOT A SINGLE "SDK ...", "StyleType constants:", "controller:",
      "apply failed:" or "remote preferences unavailable" line exists.
    - Deliberately launched a NEW Activity (Settings$WifiSettingsActivity via
      android.settings.WIFI_SETTINGS) and re-read the log page: still no new
      MagicGlass lines after 23:53:26.

  12.3 Root cause
    Hooks register without error in every scoped process but the Hooker
    callback is NEVER invoked. Two plausible causes, both addressed:
      (a) MagicOS's HwInstrumentation overrides callActivityOnCreate /
          callActivityOnResume without delegating to the stock Instrumentation
          implementation, so hooking Instrumentation.* is a dead end;
      (b) the Hooker was a private non-static inner class, which some
          LSPosed builds cannot bind a callback handle to.
    This is a real-device finding; it could not have been found offline.

  12.4 Fix shipped as 0.2.2-p1 (code 4)
    - GlassEntry.installRenderHooks(): primary hooks are now
      Activity.onResume() and Activity.onCreate(Bundle) -- every Activity must
      call super, so they are reachable regardless of Instrumentation wrappers.
      Instrumentation hooks are kept as a complementary path.
    - ActivityHook is public static final; activityFrom(Chain) prefers
      chain.getThisObject() and falls back to getArg(0).
    - deoptimize() on the lifecycle methods to defeat ART inlining.
    - One-shot "hook fired: <Executable>" diagnostic BEFORE chain.proceed(),
      so a throwing proceed() can still be distinguished from a dead hook.
    - RenderController.attach() now logs "attached to <Activity>" on success.
    - compile_check.py stubs extended (getThisObject/getExecutable/deoptimize).
    - Same signing certificate as 0.2.1-p1: an in-place update keeps the
      LSPosed enable switch and all scope ticks, no re-selection needed.

  12.5 Verification after the fix is installed (to be re-run)
    Manager log page should now show, in order:
      "hook fired: ..."                 (proves the interceptor runs)
      "SDK ... mode=HOST_UIKIT"         (proves host uikit was found)
      "StyleType constants: [NORMAL..., TRANSPARENCY..., ...]"
      "controller: attached to com.android.settings/.HWSettings"
    and the L1/L2/L3 screenshots should show glass + the level-specific
    background. If "hook fired" is still absent, the next fallback is
    Application.registerActivityLifecycleCallbacks hooked off
    Application.onCreate / ContextWrapper.attachBaseContext.

  12.6 Test discipline honoured
    Zero reboot / soft-reboot commands, zero phone-side file writes,
    zero push/install/settings put/rm, zero /sdcard writes.
    Only read-only commands (logcat -d, dumpsys, pm list packages,
    exec-out screencap to the PC, /proc reads) plus UI navigation
    (am start -W, input tap/keyevent/swipe) were used.
    uiautomator dump /dev/stdout returns only its trailer line on this ROM,
    which is why the manager UI had to be read from screenshots.

13. Real-device bring-up SUCCESS (2026-11-12 00:12 - 00:27)
------------------------------------------------------------
  User widened the permission: in-place install allowed, SOFT reboot allowed,
  hard reboot forbidden. Root available via KernelSU (/system/bin/su,
  context u:r:ksu:s0). Every reboot performed was
  `su -c 'setprop ctl.restart zygote'`; `sys.boot_completed` stayed 1 and
  zygote pid changed, proving no kernel/hardware restart. `adb reboot` was
  NEVER used. adb drops USB for ~20-30 s after a zygote restart and re-enumerates
  (transport_id 1 -> 3 -> 5); `adb wait-for-device` + `adb reconnect` recovers.

  13.1 Correction to 12.3
    The 0.2.2 log shows `hook fired: Instrumentation.callActivityOnCreate`.
    So Instrumentation WAS reachable; the real blocker was the Hooker being a
    private non-static inner class that LSPosed 2.2.1 could not bind a callback
    handle to. Making ActivityHook `public static final` fixed both hook
    families. (The new Activity.onResume/onCreate hooks are kept as extra
    coverage; the first callback still comes from Instrumentation.)

  13.2 0.2.2 -- hooks fixed, still no visual change
    Logs: hook fired -> SDK usable=true mode=HOST_UIKIT found=16 missing=3 ->
    all four StyleType constants + 15 SceneType constants (with real mode bits)
    -> controller: attached to HWSettings -> page level=L2.
    But the L1 screenshot was still plain white cards.

  13.3 Root cause of the invisible render (found via root `dumpsys activity top`)
    The decor tree was:
      DecorView
        FrameLayout(our overlay, index 0)   <- 3 HnTransFrameLayout, correct
          ImageView (backdrop, full screen)     geometry 49,822-1223,1120 etc.
          TextureView (GONE)
          FrameLayout(glassHost) -> 3 x HnTransFrameLayout
        LinearLayout(content root)          <- opaque, painted OVER the overlay
          ... HnTransparentPattern / DrawerLayoutEx / HwRecyclerView / cards
    So the scanner and container creation were CORRECT (rects=3 blocks=3
    containers=3); the stock content chain hid everything. syncRows() only
    cleared 4 ancestors and explicitly kept "transparentpattern", so it never
    reached the opaque content root (~12 views up).

  13.4 0.2.3 -- visible glass + editable background
    - MAX_ANCESTOR_DEPTH 4 -> 32; removed "transparentpattern" from
      keepBackground(); original Drawables are still saved/restored.
    - Filtered androidx.lifecycle.ReportFragment from the level signal.
    - Added scan/cleared diagnostics.
    Result: `backgrounds cleared: visible=23 tracked=23`; screenshots show the
    user's image as full-page background and 3 translucent glass card blocks,
    text and icons still crisp, no ghosting or misalignment.
    L2 (Settings$WirelessSettingsActivity, taskIndex=1) = L2 conf=90;
    L3 (SubSettings, taskIndex=2) = L3 conf=90;
    deepest L3 (Settings$TetherSettingsActivity, taskIndex=3) = L3 conf=90.
    The deepest page shows EACH standalone card glassed individually, which is
    the requested "separated cards get their own glass".

  13.5 0.2.4 -- final polish (installed, verified)

      must not demote it: added `!looksL1` to the demotion condition.
    - Noise fragments: ignore any androidx.* fragment as well.

      3<->8 blocks while a list settles, previously 30 Hz log flood).
    Final log: `page level=L1 conf=95 taskIndex=0 ... | slot=l1 |
    mode=HOST_UIKIT`, NO subsequent L2 demotion; one scan line only.

14. "Level-1 menu residue" -- root cause and fix (0.2.5-p1)
================================================================================
14.1 Symptom
  The stock white level-1 dashboard intermittently comes back over the glass
  (whole page, or a few rows inside an otherwise glass card), then the glass
  rebuilds a second or two later. Most visible when the page is IDLE, and on
  L1 -> L2 -> L1 transitions.

14.2 Evidence (LSPosed modules_*.log, read with root)
  BEFORE (0.2.4-p1), with no touch at all:
    00:34:10.548  scan: rects=0 blocks=0 containers=0
    00:34:10.548  backgrounds cleared: visible=0 tracked=0   <-- all restored
    00:34:12.526  scan: rects=3 blocks=3 containers=0        <-- glass deleted
    00:34:12.527  backgrounds cleared: visible=18 tracked=18
    00:34:13.503  scan: rects=3 blocks=3 containers=0        <-- happens again
  A pre-draw scan intermittently returns ZERO rows (window relayout / list
  rebind / transition). scanAndBind() then:
    (a) syncRows(empty) restores EVERY saved OEM white row background
        (tracked drops 20 -> 0), and
    (b) the `while (containers.size() > rects.size())` loop removes ALL glass
        containers in the same frame.
  So the page flashes back to the untouched level-1 menu until the next scan.

  Second, independent defect: syncRows() was called AFTER
  `if (sig == lastGeoSig && containers.size() == rects.size()) return;`.
  RecyclerView rebinds rows and re-applies their white drawables even when
  geometry is unchanged, so those rows went opaque again and looked like
  left-over native cards, contradicting the method's own comment.

14.3 Fix (RenderController.java)
  - MAX_TRANSIENT_EMPTY_SCANS = 30 (~1 s at 32 ms/scan): an empty scan while
    containers exist now KEEPS the last good frame (containers + stripped
    backgrounds) and returns early. Only a sustained empty run tears down.
    emptyScanStreak is reset in clearGlass().
  - syncRows(blocks) moved BEFORE the geometry-signature early return, so it
    runs on EVERY non-empty scan and immediately re-strips rebound white rows.
  - clearOpaqueChildren(row, visible): standalone / quick-setting cards wrap
    their content in an inner full-width container that owns the white
    drawable. Clear it too, but only for child containers covering >=75% of
    the row width and >=60% of its height; ImageView / TextView / Switch /
    Button keep their own drawables.
  - BOOT_BACKDROP path keeps its own syncRows() call.

14.4 Verification (soft reboot, zygote only; no hard reboot)
  AFTER (0.2.5-p1 / code 7):
    00:38:10.724  scan: rects=0 blocks=0 containers=0   (first attach, allowed)
    00:38:10.977  backgrounds cleared: visible=20 tracked=20
    00:38:13.005  backgrounds cleared: visible=20 tracked=20
    00:38:15.151  backgrounds cleared: visible=20 tracked=20
  No `tracked=0` tear-down after the glass exists. Three screenshots taken
  0 s / 6 s / 16 s into an idle page are byte-identical (1853476 B each).
  L1 -> L2 -> L1 round trip also keeps visible=19/20 tracked=19/20 with no
  white flash, and the returned L1 matches the idle frame.

14.5 Clarification on the pale upper rows
  A side-by-side crop of the stock page vs 0.2.5 shows the WLAN / Bluetooth
  rows DO show the wallpaper pattern through the glass. They only look white
  because the custom image is pale in that region plus the liquid-glass
  highlight. This is NOT an opaque native card residue.

14.6 Remaining, separate transition-frame effect
  The very first frame of an L1 -> L2 transition still shows a narrow strip of
  the outgoing L1 list on the left and a solid WHITE starting window on the
  right (burst2_1.png, mean colour spread 0.36 / brightness 252.6). This is
  the system starting window plus the outgoing activity's slide animation, not
  a renderer tear-down. Suppressing the white starting window is the P2 item
  `installStartingWindowHooks()` (still a stub) and needs a system_server-side
  or pre-decor hook. Not changed in 0.2.5 to avoid a risky hook addition.

15. SESSION HANDOVER / END-OF-DAY SUMMARY (2026-11-12 00:42)
================================================================================
Scope of work: 2026-11-11 21:16 - 2026-11-12 00:42. Goal: a standalone LSPosed
module (`com.magicglass`) that makes MagicOS Settings card backgrounds editable
and applies the system's own liquid glass to cards on level-1/2/3 pages.

15.1 Delivered, in order
  (1) Full reverse engineering of HonorBG.apk: upstream
      Solomonstery/HyperBackground 1.4.5-beta2 shell + Hellolimt/HonorBG 1.4.6
      bridge (classes2.dex, java_init.list, scope.list rewritten at
      2026-11-10 23:59:36; signed CN=HonorBackgroundPort). That bridge is a
      MIUI-derived base and was REJECTED as a code base per user instruction.
      No setMi* API and no com.ciallo class is referenced anywhere in our code.
  (2) Reference projects big-dictator/LiquidTab and kyant0/AndroidLiquidGlass
      (Backdrop) studied; their AGSL/RenderNode ideas kept as a DOWNGRADE path
      only, because the ROM already ships the effect natively.
  (3) Read-only device probing established: uiautomator is unusable at runtime
      (continuously animating HwProgressBar / HnGradientBlur* containers), the
      level signal is the per-task ACTIVITY STACK (not Fragment back stack), and
      card geometry must be grouped by gap, never hard-coded row heights.
  (4) DECISIVE: each scope APK ships the full internal uikit implementation
      (128 com.hihonor.uikit.transparency.* classes in Settings classes4.dex).
      Final architecture = HOST_UIKIT: instantiate the host's own
      HnTransFrameLayout with the host ClassLoader, one container per card
      block in a decor overlay, aligned from a pre-draw callback. It follows
      the system transparency slider / material switch / dark mode through its
      own onConfigurationChanged(). Fallbacks: BOOT_BACKDROP (framework
      setBackdropRenderEffect) and AGSL. Mode enum:
      NONE / HOST_UIKIT / BOOT_BACKDROP / APP_CONTAINER / DEVELOPER_SDK.
  (5) Offline disassembly (probe/dex_deep.py, deep_uikit.txt) corrected the two
      assumptions that would have killed the main path: enum FIELD names are
      obfuscated but Enum.name() is plaintext; a no-arg constructed container
      defaults to StyleType.NORMAL, which wo/f.e() rejects, so "follow" MUST
      inject TRANSPARENCY explicitly. Empty containers DO draw; setCornerRadius
      may only be called once at creation.
  (6) P1 implementation: 12 app Java files + 1 probe entry. CardScanner,
      RenderController, MaterialBridge, PageLevel, BackdropLayer (TextureView +
      MediaPlayer), ConfigBus/ConfigStore/ModuleProvider (ContentProvider +
      broadcast cross-process config), ConfigContract (mg.<slot>.<param> with
      per-parameter inherit), SettingsActivity (pure-Java UI for per-level
      parameters and background picking), GlassEntry, Scope.
  (7) Local build chain discovered in harness/hb-work/tools (aapt2, d8,
      zipalign, apksigner, android-35, api-101 jar) -> build.sh produces real
      signed APKs locally; app/libs/api-101.jar also filled for the Gradle path.
  (8) Real-device bring-up across 0.2.1 -> 0.2.5. Three root causes fixed:
      Hooker must be public static final (LSPosed 2.2.1 binding); the overlay
      was at DecorView index 0 under ~12 opaque ancestors (ancestor clearing
      4 -> 32, remove transparentpattern from keepBackground); transient empty
      scans tore the whole glass down (MAX_TRANSIENT_EMPTY_SCANS=30, syncRows
      before the geometry early-return, clearOpaqueChildren).
  (9) Verified on device: L1/L2/L3 + deepest "more share settings" pages all
      show semi-transparent glass cards over the editable background with
      legible text; level detection L1 conf=95 / L2 conf=90 / L3 conf=90,
      taskIndex 0/1/2/3. Standalone cards each get their own glass container.

15.3 Rules of engagement (confirmed by the user)
  ALLOWED: `adb install -r` (in-place update), PURE SOFT REBOOT via
  `su -c 'setprop ctl.restart zygote'`, read-only dumpsys / pm / logcat /
  exec-out screencap to PC, UI navigation (am start -W, input tap/keyevent).
  FORBIDDEN: any hard reboot / `adb reboot`; anything else that writes system
  state. After a soft reboot sys.boot_completed stays 1, only the zygote pid
  changes; USB re-enumerates for 20-30 s (wait-for-device, then adb reconnect).
  LSPosed 2.2.1 does NOT forward this module's XposedModule.log() to logcat and
  Log.i is also invisible there: read module logs with root from
  /data/adb/lspd/log/modules_*.log (or the manager's Logs page).
  `uiautomator dump /dev/stdout` returns only its trailer on this ROM; long
  `input text` strings get reordered by the IME (short words like "magic" work).

16.1 New tool: probe/scope_audit.py
  Resolves DEX class_def items (not string greps) for all 8 offline scope APKs

  probe/probe-report/scope_audit.txt.

  scope                    dex  uikit  frame  style  style-param        legacy        dynamic
  com.android.settings       4    128   YES    YES    ...StyleType;      HOST_UIKIT    HOST_UIKIT
  com.hihonor.systemmanager  3    101   YES    -      Lqe8;              BOOT_BACKDROP HOST_UIKIT

  com.android.systemui       4    249   YES    YES    ...StyleType;      HOST_UIKIT    HOST_UIKIT

  DECISIVE: systemmanager / wallet define the uikit container but ship a
  DIFFERENTLY OBFUSCATED StyleType (Lqe8; / Ls29;) and no plain class name.
  Their setViewStyleType(Lqe8;) / setViewStyleWithScenes(...) methods are intact.

  constants are STILL plaintext: NORMAL, TRANSPARENCY,

  => The old hard-coded CLS_STYLE_TYPE lookup needlessly downgraded those two
     scopes to BOOT_BACKDROP. ALL 8 scopes can use HOST_UIKIT.

16.2 Code changes (0.2.6-p1 / versionCode 8)
  MaterialBridge.java
    - New resolveEnums(): try the plain class names first; otherwise walk the
      declared methods of HnTransFrameLayout / HnTransRelativeLayout /
      HnTransLinearLayout / HnMaterialAggregator and collect enum parameter
      types. An enum whose constants contain TRANSPARENCY+NORMAL is StyleType;
      one containing General/BigWidget/Immersive is SceneType. Cached per
      process and reset at the start of probe().
    - probe() now decides HOST_UIKIT from the RESOLVED style class, not the
      hard-coded name; frame check accepts any of the three container classes.
      Availability.describe() now logs the actual (possibly obfuscated) class
      names, so the next on-device run proves the resolution.
    - describeStyleTypes / resolveStyle / sceneMask / applyHostStyle all use
      the resolved classes. New enumModeStrict() finds a power-of-two int mode
      field (the field NAME may be obfuscated a/b/c); when it cannot, it
      returns -1 and the caller falls back to setViewStyleType() instead of
      injecting a wrong ordinal-derived scene mask.
  RenderController.java (P2 geometry jitter)
    - GEOMETRY_STABLE_FRAMES = 3 (~96 ms). Only the CONTAINER POOL SIZE is
      damped: a target count must repeat for 3 scans before containers are
      added/removed. Positions are still applied every scan, so scrolling is
      unaffected. While the count is in flux, surplus containers are set
      INVISIBLE instead of being detached, and syncRows() still runs every
      scan. clearGlass() resets the hysteresis state.
    - This fixes the 3 <-> 8 block churn seen during L1 -> L2 transitions.
  GlassEntry.java (P0 design only, NO behaviour change)
    - installStartingWindowHooks() stays a no-op but now carries the full
      hook-point design (see 16.4).
  probe/compile_check.py: View stub gains INVISIBLE = 4.

16.3 P2a (L3 help paragraph) -- ORIGINAL HYPOTHESIS REFUTED, NOT CHANGED
  The 13.7 note suggested adding "the help container class" to keepBackground().
  Offline evidence disproves it:
    - com.android.settings.widget.SummaryPreferenceLayout is NOT a help
      container. Both saved View Hierarchy dumps show it as a normal
      dashboard/list row with icon, title, summary and arrow (id/dashboard_tile).
      Whitelisting it would restore opaque white cards -- the exact regression
      fixed in section 14.
    - keepBackground() is only consulted for ANCESTORS. Row backgrounds go
      through saveAndClear(row) unconditionally, so a class whitelist could not
      protect the paragraph anyway.
  => Do not touch the rendering chain without a fresh on-device
     `su -c 'dumpsys activity top'` dump of the deepest L3 page. First item
     after USB is reconnected.

16.4 P0 (white transition first frame) -- hook points located, still disabled
  A system_server hook is NOT required. Offline DEX audit located the entire
  starting-window implementation INSIDE com.android.systemui.apk, which is
  already one of our 9 scopes -- i.e. exactly the process that calls the
  installStartingWindowHooks() branch. Real classes in classes3.dex:
    com.android.wm.shell.ShellTaskOrganizer
        .addStartingWindow(android.window.StartingWindowInfo)       [earliest]
    com.android.wm.shell.startingsurface.StartingWindowController
        .addStartingWindow(android.window.StartingWindowInfo)       [dispatch]
    com.android.wm.shell.startingsurface.HnSplashscreenWindowCreator
        .addSplashScreenStartingWindow(StartingWindowInfo, int)
        .estimateTaskBackgroundColor(android.app.TaskInfo) -> int   [COLOR]
    com.android.wm.shell.startingsurface.SplashscreenContentDrawer
        .ColorCache.WindowColor                                     [theme]
  framework.jar only has the client-side android.window.StartingWindowInfo /
  SplashScreen / ITaskOrganizer.addStartingWindow; no server controller class
  (it lives in services.jar, which was not pulled).
  Safest option: hook estimateTaskBackgroundColor(TaskInfo) and return a colour
  matching the configured backdrop, preserving the whole splash animation.
  MANDATORY guard: filter by StartingWindowInfo.targetActivityInfo
  .packageName / TaskInfo.baseIntent, otherwise every app's launch screen is
  changed. Hooker must be public static final. Not implemented until the user
  confirms the "residue" they meant is this first frame.

18. 0.3.x FRAME-PIPELINE REWRITE + SYSTEMUI P0 + EXTERNAL SCOPES
    (2026-11-12 10:25 - 10:55, device connected, Agent mode)
================================================================================
USER DECISIONS THIS TURN
  (1) "Level-1 cards stutter / leave residue when scrolling up and down;
      switching to a level-2 menu makes the glass disappear or turn back into
      white cards. A SystemUI hook is fine as long as it does NOT touch the
      original system files."
  (2) "Include external scopes in the adaptation. Requirement: stable and
      smooth. You decide the approach. You may also continue with the TODOs."
  => The reported "residue" is NOT the white starting window alone; it is a
     combination of THREE defects. Both were fixed. SystemUI hook enabled
     under a strict runtime-only / package-guard contract.

18.1 REPRODUCTION (0.2.7-p1 / code 9, before any change)
  repro_L2_frame0.png: ENTIRELY WHITE screen, only the status bar. That is the
  ROM starting window, not a card. repro_L2_frame1.png (parentcontrol Home)
  shows opaque white cards over a visible custom wallpaper.
  repro_L1_after.png: after a fast fling, the top "情景模式" tile is a paler,
  near-opaque card and a previous row's text is visible under the translucent
  title bar; glass and list are out of sync.
  Module log showed the smoking gun repeatedly:
      controller: scan: rects=3 blocks=3 containers=0
      controller: scan: rects=4 blocks=4 containers=0
      controller: scan: rects=5 blocks=5 containers=4
  i.e. cards were found but ZERO glass containers existed for several frames.
  gfxinfo (974 frames): 95th 32ms / 99th 121ms / 31 slow-UI-thread frames.

18.2 ROOT CAUSES (five, all confirmed in code / on-device logs)
  A. ConfigContract.glassFingerprint() included slot.id. Every L1 -> L2
     transition therefore looked like a MATERIAL change, so refresh() called
     clearGlass() and destroyed the whole container pool.
  B. Refresh(force=true) was invoked by EVERY onLifecycle() (four hooks are
     installed, so one Activity start reaches it several times) and force was
     part of the `if (glassChanged || force)` rebuild condition -> repeated
     tear-downs with no material change at all.
  C. GEOMETRY_STABLE_FRAMES=3 delayed pool growth by ~96 ms after every
     tear-down, so `rects=3 / containers=0` was visible for many frames.
  D. An empty scan held the LAST geometry for MAX_TRANSIENT_EMPTY_SCANS=30
     (~1 s) and skipped syncRows(). During a fling this froze glass rectangles
     at stale positions ("residue") AND skipped stripping OEM white drawables
     on transition frames ("turns back into white cards").
  E. PERFORMANCE / ONE-FRAME LAG:
     - MIN_SCAN_INTERVAL_MS=32 => glass updated at ~30 fps while the list
       scrolled at 120 fps.
     - MaterialBridge.configure() called setLayoutParams() from onPreDraw().
       onPreDraw runs AFTER layout, so the new params only take effect in the
       NEXT frame's layout pass: glass trailed the list by one full frame.
     - syncRows() walked 32 ancestors + all row children on EVERY frame and
       called setBackground(null); glassHost.invalidate() every geometry frame.

18.3 FIXES (0.3.0-p1 / code 10, then 0.3.1-p1 / code 11)
  ConfigContract.java
    - glassFingerprint() no longer contains slot.id; resolve() already returns
      the actual per-slot parameter VALUES. Added Param GLASS_GENERIC_CARDS
      ("glass.genericCards", default true); SettingsActivity enumerates
      Param.values() so the switch appears in the UI automatically.
  Scope.java
    - isExternal(), isStartingWindowTarget() (settings + 6 external only).
  MaterialBridge.java (configure)
    - Silently updates FrameLayout.LayoutParams fields AND calls
      layout.layout(l,t,r,b) for THIS frame. setLayoutParams() is never called
      from pre-draw. Added a full no-op fast path when frame + params match.
  CardScanner.java
    - Scanner.visibleRows() exposes EVERY qualifying row (not only rows that
      formed a block), so white stripping is decoupled from grouping.
    - New guarded GENERIC path for bespoke external-app cards, opt-in via
      glass.genericCards AND Scope.isExternal() (never used in Settings).
      Guards: depth<=18, <=14 cards, w>=0.18*screen, h>=110px, area<=58%
      screen, total<=90%, overlap<=60%, chrome/control class+resource name
      blacklist, outermost-of-nesting-chain, our own overlay subtree skipped.
    - genericCache (WeakReference) + 1.5 s periodic re-discovery. CRITICAL:
      the detector identifies a card BY HAVING A BACKGROUND, but syncRows()
      strips that very drawable. Without the cache it forgot its own cards on
      the next frame (0.3.0 symptom: white cards removed, no glass built).
  RenderController.java (full frame-pipeline rewrite)
    - Empty frames hide glass containers but NEVER restore OEM backgrounds.
    - Pool grows immediately, is reused, never removes Views; surplus
      containers -> INVISIBLE; hard ceiling MAX_POOL_SIZE=20.
    - Adaptive throttle: every frame while geometry is moving, 64 ms idle.
    - Expensive 32-level ancestor walk / child-wrapper search cached behind
      an identity signature of the visible row set; cheap idempotent
      re-strip still runs each frame (RecyclerView rebind protection).
    - onLifecycle() debounced 180 ms and calls refresh(false).
    - clearGlass() only on a REAL material change.
    - ensureObserver(): a window rebuild replaces decor.getViewTreeObserver().
      0.3.0 removed the listener from the tree that was DISPATCHING onPreDraw,
      which silently killed the callback chain (one "reattached" line then no
      controller logs at all in external apps). 0.3.1 only ADDS listeners and
      keeps registeredTrees; removal happens exclusively in detach(), never
      mid-dispatch. Duplicate registration is harmless (scan is throttled).
    - glassUnavailable latch: if createLayout() fails, restoreRows() and stop
      stripping rather than leaving unreadable text on the wallpaper.
  BackdropLayer.java
    - clearWindowBackground(): saves the DecorView's opaque window drawable
      and sets it to null AT RUNTIME (no /system write, no APK edit, no file
      write); restoreWindowBackground() in hide()/release(). Fixes the wallet
      scope where the whole overlay was hidden behind the OEM window background.
  GlassEntry.java (P0 ENABLED)
    - installStartingWindowHooks(ClassLoader) uses param.getDefaultClassLoader()
      and pure reflection, all wrapped, never breaks systemui.
    - PRIMARY: StartingWindowController.addStartingWindow -> skip (correct
      zero value per return type) ONLY when the launch package is a scope.
    - FALLBACK: HnSplashscreenWindowCreator.estimateTaskBackgroundColor ->
      return Color.TRANSPARENT for scoped packages.
    - startingPackage() resolves targetActivityInfo.packageName /
      taskInfo.topActivityInfo / baseIntent.getPackage/getComponent. If the
      package cannot be resolved, chain.proceed() leaves the ROM untouched.
    - ShellTaskOrganizer deliberately NOT hooked.
    - Both Hookers are public static final (LSPosed 2.2.1 requirement).
  probe/compile_check.py
    - Stubs gained View.getLeft/getTop/getWidth/getHeight/layout() and
      PackageLoadedParam.getDefaultClassLoader().

18.4 QUALITY GATE (both builds)
  COMPILE_OK / 131 class files (13 sources, 62 stubs, real javac);
  JAVALANG_OK 12 files; bash -n; py_compile on 5 tools; 9 scopes; both
  Manifest XML parse; zero MIUI setMi*/com.ciallo CODE refs (2 comments only);
  build.sh six-step v2+v3 signing with CN=MagicGlass; apksigner verify.
  0.3.0-p1: 53,673 B SHA256 f861ff8d...7d82 / code 10.
  0.3.1-p1: 53,673 B SHA256 d35b1809...6be6 / code 11 (FINAL, installed).
  Older APKs preserved in build-out/ and probe/probe-report/archive/.

18.5 ON-DEVICE SESSION (all restrictions honoured)
  Device A4UF6R6116003189 / AAK-AN00. Two same-signature `adb install -r`
  and TWO PURE SOFT REBOOTS (`su -c 'setprop ctl.restart zygote'`).
  zygote64: 13922 -> 4441 -> 30364; sys.boot_completed stayed 1 throughout.
  NO `adb reboot`, NO hard reboot, NO writes to the phone filesystem.

18.6 VERIFIED RESULTS (0.3.1-p1)
  P0 SystemUI: log `hooked StartingWindowController.addStartingWindow`,
  `hooked HnSplashscreenWindowCreator.estimateTaskBackgroundColor`,
  `starting-window suppression: skip=true colorFallback=true`.

  NO other app was affected. 031_L2_frame0.png and 030_L2_frame0.png are
  transition frames with real content + glass, NOT the 35 KB pure-white frame
  of repro_L2_frame0.png.
  Glass loss: settings log now `rects=4 blocks=4 containers=4 rows=14` and
  `rects=3 blocks=3 containers=3`; the `containers=0` state is gone.
  030_L2_settled.png / 031_L1_final.png: every card semi-transparent glass,
  including the top tile that used to be white; no white card residue.
  Fling: 030_L1_after.png all glass and aligned; after-vs-idle 2 s pixel
  change 0.000% (0.3.0) / 1.046% (0.3.1, status-bar clock only).
  031_L1_final.png top "情景模式" card is glass, not solid white.
  External scopes (generic=true, mode=HOST_UIKIT):
    parentcontrol HomeActivity  : big mode card + 2 twin cards glass,
        wallpaper visible, log visible=16 tracked=16 ancestors=13.
        Evidence 031_EXT_parentcontrol.png (vs 030_ = text on wallpaper).
    systemmanager MainScreen    : anti-fraud card + 3 function cards glass,
        wallpaper visible, log visible=24 tracked=24 ancestors=17.
        Evidence 031_EXT_systemmanager.png.
    wallet WalletHomeActivity  : background now no longer solid white but the
        custom image still does NOT show through; the page is an icon grid
        with no real card Views (only the bottom bar is glass-like).
        Evidence 031_EXT_wallet.png. Log visible=16 tracked=16.
  Module config UI opens normally: global slot, bg.source =
  bg_image_1794412445661.jpg, all 6 buttons. Evidence 031_config_ui.png.

================================================================================
19. FOLLOW-THE-FINGER, FORCED GLASS BACKING, AND THE "IS IT DELAYED
    RENDERING?" QUESTION  (2026-11-12 11:01 - 11:26, device connected)
================================================================================
USER QUESTIONS THIS TURN
  (1) "L1 cards do not follow the finger when scrolling; it looks like
      stutter."
  (2) "Adding a guard that restores the OEM white background when an
      external app cannot create a glass container -- won't that cause
      inconsistency? What about FORCING a glass backing instead?"
  (3) "Are these residue problems caused by delayed rendering?"
  (4) "Continue the unfinished work."
  Answers: (1) FIXED in 0.3.2/0.3.3. (2) The user is RIGHT, the guard was
  WRONG -- replaced with a forced translucent backing. (3) PARTLY, and the
  measurement is below. (4) Done for the three named items; remaining TODOs
  listed in 19.8 were deliberately NOT bundled to avoid regressing a freshly
  stabilised frame pipeline.

19.1 BASELINE: 0.3.1 IS NOT ACTUALLY DROPPING FRAMES
  Identical workload on device A4UF6R6116003189 (start HWSettings, wait,
  `input swipe 636 2300 636 600 120`, reverse swipe, NO screencap during the
  gesture so the capture does not block RenderThread):
    0.3.1-p1  100 frames  janky 6.00%  50th 17ms  95th 31ms  99th 40ms
  Only 6% janky and a 17 ms median. The user's "stutter" therefore CANNOT be
  primarily a rendering-speed problem: the glass was simply FROZEN at stale
  positions for several frames. This is the key measurement behind the 19.3
  answer.

19.2 ROOT CAUSE OF "NOT FOLLOWING THE FINGER" (0.3.1 -> 0.3.2)
  RenderController.onPreDraw():
      long interval = moving ? 0L : SCAN_INTERVAL_IDLE_MS;  // 64 ms
  `moving` is an OUTPUT of the PREVIOUS scan (sig != lastGeoSig), so:
    - the first ~64 ms (4 frames @60 Hz / 8 @120 Hz) of EVERY fling ran with
      the glass at the previous position -> visible "lag" at gesture start;
    - at high refresh rates a fling often moves <1 px/frame, so the rounded
      rect signature repeated, `moving` flipped back to false, and the 64 ms
      throttle cut in MID-GESTURE -> visible "stutter";
    - an empty scan still held the last geometry (section 17/18 residue).
  Secondary costs: syncRows() allocated a HashSet and swept savedRows on
  EVERY frame; MaterialBridge.configure() called layout() on every container
  on every frame (setFrame + invalidate cascade), even for a pure vertical
  shift.

19.3 ANSWER TO QUESTION 3: "IS IT DELAYED RENDERING?"
  PARTLY -- but the delayed part is DETECTION, not GPU rendering.
    YES, delayed:
      a) the 64 ms `moving` feedback loop above (a delayed / stale INPUT);
      b) historically setLayoutParams() from onPreDraw() only took effect in
         the NEXT frame's layout pass -- fixed in 0.3.0 (section 18.2 E).
    NO, architectural (the larger part):
      c) the glass containers live in a DecorView OVERLAY, i.e. a completely
         different View / RenderNode from the RecyclerView. Their positions
         must be recomputed in onPreDraw and pushed every frame. This is one
         extra CPU pass + one extra compositor layer compared with a card
         that simply owns its own background. It is a SYNCHRONISATION problem,
         not a latency problem.
      d) re-drawing the host liquid-glass RenderEffect on every container
         every frame costs real GPU time (0.3.2 regression below).
  Proof: 0.3.1 had almost no jank (6.00%, 17 ms median) yet visibly lagged.
  A truly "slow renderer" would show high jank, not frozen-but-smooth frames.
  The only complete architectural cure is to make the glass a background /
  child of each row View so it scrolls WITH the RecyclerView (zero per-frame
  position maths). That abandons the host's native liquid-glass material,
  which the user explicitly chose (route A). We therefore keep HOST_UIKIT and
  remove the detection lag + per-frame cost instead.

19.4 0.3.2-p1 (code 12) -- ZERO-LAG, EVERY-FRAME ALIGNMENT
  - Removed the `moving ? 0 : 64ms` throttle. Only a 4 ms de-dup for duplicate
    OnPreDrawListener registrations remained.
  - MaterialBridge.configure(): SDK-only reflection moved into its OWN try
    (a fallback View has no setRadius/setBound). Pure vertical motion now uses
    View.offsetTopAndBottom(delta) -- an animation-grade RenderNode shift with
    no measure/layout cascade -- instead of layout(). Full fast path retained.
  - syncRows(): HashSet allocation, savedRows sweep, 32-level ancestor walk
    and child-wrapper search now run ONLY when the row-set identity signature
    changes; steady frames do only cheap idempotent re-stripping.
  - Frame diagnostics added: `frames: total=N scanned=N motion=N aligned=N
    maxGapMs=N fallback=N`.
  VERIFIED: `frames: total=488 scanned=242 aligned=237` -> 98% of scanned
  frames realigned the glass; 032_L1_mid.png shows glass exactly on the text
  rows, no smear, no white card; settle stability 0.391%.
  REGRESSION MEASURED (3 runs): janky 6.93/7.00/7.84%, 50th 20-21 ms,
  95th 28-38 ms. ~+3 ms median vs 0.3.1 -- the cost of scanning idle frames.

19.5 0.3.3-p1 (code 13, FINAL) -- MOTION-GATED, ZERO-LAG SCHEDULING
  CardScanner.Scanner.quickToken(): hashes raw getTop() of the first 4 and
  the last child of each cached RecyclerView plus cached generic cards.
  No Rect allocation, no tree walk, no native call. It is read IN THE CURRENT
  frame BEFORE deciding whether to run the full scan:
      boolean motion = (lastQuickToken == MIN) || (token != lastQuickToken);
      if (!motion && !pendingFullScan && idle < 64ms) return;  // idle: free
      ... full scanAndBind()                                   // motion: every frame
  This has NO feedback loop (input, not previous output), so the first frame
  of a fling is detected with zero lag, while a genuinely idle page skips
  ~80% of full scans. refresh()/onLifecycle() set pendingFullScan=true so a
  config change or window rebuild always forces a full scan.
  VERIFIED 3 runs, same workload:
      run1 110 frames  janky 6.36%  50th 19ms  95th 30ms  99th 34ms
      run2 102 frames  janky 8.82%  50th 15ms  95th 22ms  99th 23ms
      run3 101 frames  janky 6.93%  50th 16ms  95th 27ms  99th 31ms
    50th percentile back to 16 ms (median of 3), i.e. AT or BELOW the 0.3.1
    17 ms baseline, while alignment is still every-frame.
    `frames: total=182 scanned=38 motion=22 aligned=37` -> 79% idle frames
    skipped; `total=46 scanned=7 motion=0` -> a fully idle window is nearly
    free. 033_L1_mid.png: exact alignment, no smear, no white card.
    after-vs-idle 2 s = 1.032% (status-bar clock only).

19.6 ANSWER TO QUESTION 2: THE WHITE-BACKGROUND GUARD WAS WRONG
  0.3.1 used a `glassUnavailable` latch: if createLayout() returned null it
  called restoreRows() and stopped rendering for the Activity's lifetime.
  Concrete failure modes (the user's "错乱" intuition was correct):
    - a SINGLE transient failure (ClassLoader not warm, one reflection miss)
      permanently downgraded the whole Activity to opaque white cards;
    - glass <-> white flipping on a flaky SDK, and a mixed state if
      restoreRows() disagreed with the current scan's visible row set;
    - it re-introduced exactly the "white card regression" fixed in 18.2;
    - it still left text on the bare wallpaper if restoreRows() ran late.
  0.3.2 REPLACED it. New FallbackGlass.java: a plain View with a rounded
  translucent GradientDrawable (~80% opaque, night-mode aware, ~1 px stroke,
  radius from geo.radiusDp). No host uikit, no reflection, no render layer,
  always constructible. ensurePool() switches the ENTIRE pool to it and
  `fallbackMode` is LATCHED for the Activity lifetime (hysteresis: no
  oscillation). OEM backgrounds are NEVER restored except on detach or an
  explicit glass-off. `MaterialBridge.configure()` still aligns these Views.
  New Param GLASS_FORCE_BACKING ("glass.forceBacking", default false) lets
  the user select this path deliberately; SettingsActivity enumerates
  Param.values() so the row appears automatically.
  ON-DEVICE A/B (via the module's own config UI, no adb file writes):
    false -> true: log `host glass unavailable -> forced translucent backing
    (latched)`, `frames: ... fallback=true`, NO `construction failed`.
    032_forcebacking_L1.png: every card has a readable translucent backing,
    wallpaper visible, NO white card, NO text-on-wallpaper; idle 2 s pixel
    change 0.024%.
    true -> false: log `material changed -> retry host glass once`,
    `fallback=false`. 032_restored_L1.png: native liquid glass returns, NO
    mixed state (no half-glass/half-flat cards), NO white flash, NO breakage.
  => Forced glass backing is strictly safer than restoring white. Adopted.

19.7 QUALITY GATE, INSTALL CHAIN, RESTRICTIONS
  Both builds: COMPILE_OK / 134 class files (14 sources, 64 stubs, real
  javac); JAVALANG_OK 13 files; XML_OK AndroidManifest; bash -n; py_compile
  on 5 probe tools; 9 scopes; zero MIUI setMi*/com.ciallo CODE refs (2
  explanatory comments only); build.sh six-step v2+v3 signing with
  CN=MagicGlass; apksigner verify.
    0.3.2-p1  53,673 B  SHA256 821e5de4...6cee  code 12
    0.3.3-p1  53,673 B  SHA256 872ae156...c256  code 13 (FINAL, installed)
  Older APKs preserved in build-out/ and probe/probe-report/archive/.
  Two same-signature `adb install -r` and TWO PURE SOFT REBOOTS
  (`su -c 'setprop ctl.restart zygote'`). zygote64 30364 -> 9870 -> 9777;
  sys.boot_completed stayed 1. NO `adb reboot`, NO hard reboot, NO /system
  writes, NO adb writes to the phone filesystem. The forceBacking A/B was
  performed through the module's OWN SettingsActivity UI (normal module
  function). USB re-enumeration after the second soft reboot took ~29 s and
  needed `adb reconnect`; the LSPosed switch and 9 scopes survived both.
  Final regression (0.3.3): 033_L2_frame0.png is a real transition frame
  with glass, not the 35 KB pure-white starting window; SystemUI guard audit
  shows skips for ONLY 4 whitelisted packages (settings 7, parentcontrol 6,
  systemmanager 4, wallet 2). 033_EXT_parentcontrol.png / systemmanager.png:
  cards glass, wallpaper visible. Wallet remains the known icon-grid limit.
  Phone restored to HWSettings, versionCode=13.

19.8 REMAINING TODO (unchanged priority, deliberately NOT bundled)
  P1 - wallet custom wallpaper: an app-level opaque layer (not DecorView)
       still hides it. Needs a per-app View-Hierarchy dump; do NOT brute-force

  P2 - P2a L3 help paragraph legibility (17.6): LinearLayout

20. 0.3.4 FAST-FLING FIX, P2a BACKING SAGA, AND THE p8 JANK REGRESSION
================================================================================

20.1 0.3.4 tracking tail -- FIXED AND USER-CONFIRMED
  0.3.3 gated scans on CardScanner.Scanner.quickToken(), which hashed only
  the first 4 + last child's integer getTop(). At 120 Hz a fast fling moves
  <1 px/frame and item animations move rows via float translationY, so the
  token legitimately repeated while geometry changed: the glass froze.
  0.3.4 = (a) hardened token: list getScrollY(), float getTranslationY(),
  up to 24 children, generic cache Views; (b) a tracking tail -- detected
  motion opens `trackUntilMs = now + perf.trackTailMs` (default 160 ms,
  capped 1000 ms) in which EVERY frame runs scanAndBind() unconditionally.
  Diagnostic line extended with tail= and stall=; stall counts frames whose
  token did NOT change but geometry DID (exactly what 0.3.3 froze).
  On-device: frames total=480 scanned=465 motion=150 aligned=226 tail=314
  stall=76. Idle still scans only 4/64 frames. gfxinfo (pure swipe, NO
  screencap): janky 3.20% / 0.00% / 0.00%, 50th 23/24/24 ms.
  USER CONFIRMED: "快速滑动这个改好了已经跟手了".

20.2 P2a L3 help-paragraph backing -- p1..p6
  Target verified by `dumpsys activity top` (040_view_top_p6.txt):
    LinearLayout 112,1542-1160,2399 (help block)
      HwTextView #1020010 android:id/summary  0,105-1048,857  (h=752)
    third HnTransFrameLayout = 161,1556-1111,2385 (829 px, exactly the block)
    ordinary rows' #1020010 summary is only ~80 px high.
  p1 STANDALONE-height / p2 tallest-row / p3 whole-block+long-text all
  flickered (wanted 1 -> 0) because RecyclerView splits the 857 px paragraph
  into ~294 px children once layout settles. p4 moved denseLayer ABOVE
  glassLayer and used the ~80% FallbackGlass.createView(). p5 tried content
  addressing and failed: HwTextView is an Honor custom widget and does NOT
  extend android.widget.TextView (paragraphH=0px). p6 switched to framework
  id 0x01020010 (android:id/summary) + height >= 400 px -> wanted=1
  paragraphH=829px. Screenshots 034..039, dumps 038/040, log
  modules_0.3.4_p6.full.log.
  HwTextView / geometry / detection were NOT the remaining blocker: the
  p6 dump DOES show android.view.View{13538f9 V.ED 161,1556-1111,2385}
  inside denseLayer, i.e. the backing was attached at the correct size.

20.3 p7 root cause: TRAVERSAL STARVATION (not size, not attachment)
  ViewTreeObserver.OnPreDrawListener fires ONLY during a draw traversal.
  A perfectly static page produces NO traversal, so scanAndBind() stops and
  the paragraph detector never gets enough chances. Device proof in the p6
  log: at 12:30:44 the user was SCROLLING (frames total=460 scanned=460)
  -> wanted=1 paragraphH=829px; at 12:34:41 the user opened L3 and stopped
  -> ~10 frames, last dense log 12:34:42 wanted=0, then NO controller logs
  at all. 039_L3_dense_p6.png (12:34:46) therefore shows no backing, while
  040_view_top_p6.txt (12:35:17) shows it finally attached.
  p7 added: real-size FrameLayout.LayoutParams via lpFor(RectF) instead of
  a 1x1 placeholder; parent self-heal on every sync; a time-bounded
  lastDenseRect latch (400 ms) so a transient detach cannot blink the
  backing off; a finite main-thread kick chain (120/320/700/1400/2600 ms)
  so discovery runs even without traversals.

20.4 p8 -- USER REPORT "这个版本滑动卡顿问题又出现了"
  ROOT CAUSE (p5/p6 regression, amplified by p7):
  findDenseParagraph(decor, 0) was called from INSIDE denseTargets() whenever
  the cache was empty (every 200 ms). denseTargets() runs on EVERY scanned
  frame; the 0.3.4 tracking tail makes a fling scan almost every frame, so
  an UNBOUNDED recursive walk of the whole DecorView tree (depth <= 24, no
  node budget) landed on the main thread repeatedly DURING the gesture.
  p7 made it worse by calling the full scanAndBind() (rects() + syncRows()
  32-level ancestor walks) 5 extra times from the kick chain.
  p8 FIX:
   - CardScanner.denseTargets() is now STRICTLY O(1): it only reads the
     cached View's getGlobalVisibleRect() and the bounded last-good rect.
   - New CardScanner.discoverDense() owns the tree walk, with a hard
     DISCOVERY_NODE_BUDGET = 600 nodes; called ONLY from the kick chain.
   - denseKick no longer calls scanAndBind(); it calls denseKickSync()
     (discover + syncDense for the ONE backing at its real size), and is
     SKIPPED entirely while `now < trackUntilMs` (i.e. during a fling), so
     no tree walk can occur mid-gesture. Chain remains finite and is
     cancelled in detach().
  This also finally implements the user's "直接调整下面的底衬的大小不行吗":
  the backing reuses the already-computed 161,1556-1111,2385 rect and gets
  its exact width/height/left/top at creation.

20.5 IMPORTANT DEPLOYMENT CAVEAT -- LSPosed dex cache
  After p7 `adb install -r` + PURE SOFT REBOOT: device base.apk SHA256 =
  2087a98a...738f (byte-identical to local p7), versionName=0.3.4-p7 /
  versionCode=20, zygote64 15253 -> 18286, sys.boot_completed stayed 1.
  BUT the 12:49-12:50 module log contains ZERO "attach=" (p7-only format)
  and still prints p6's "minH=630px" -> the LSPosed DAEMON (started 09:55,
  NOT restarted by a zygote soft reboot) was still executing p6 bytecode.
  So the jank the user felt may have been p6, not p7. Before A/B testing a
  new build, force LSPosed to reload the module (toggle the module switch
  off/on, then soft-restart) and confirm the log format changed.
  NO hard reboot, NO /system writes, NO adb writes to the phone filesystem
  in this session. Screenshots/logs were read to the PC only.

21. 0.3.5-p1 -- FIVE-ITEM USER FEEDBACK: ONE ROOT CAUSE, THREE FIXES
================================================================================

21.1 USER FEEDBACK (2026-11-12 13:12, verbatim)
  "1.快速滑动基本修复正常，略微还是有些卡顿。
   2.滑完停手静置 2-3 秒，卡片有些掉帧的感觉，慢慢拖动时掉帧感变明显。
   3.模块自带配置页面过于复杂，需要中文设置界面
   4.点进个人热点最下面的卡片底衬有白卡闪一下后消失，现在显示出的玻璃卡
     可以根据上面玻璃卡的大小方式进行更改。
   5切页返回目前正常。"
  Item 5 (L1->L2->L3->back glass continuity) is CONFIRMED GOOD, no change made.

21.2 ONE ROOT CAUSE EXPLAINS 1, 2 AND 4
  All three symptoms trace back to the p7/p8 P2a discovery machinery:
  - denseKick delays were {120, 320, 700, 1400, 2600} ms. Item 2's
    "滑完停手静置 2-3 秒" is EXACTLY this window: after the finger lifted,
    the kick chain ran discoverDense() = a 600-node DecorView recursion on
    the main looper, 5 times. -> post-gesture dropped frames.
  - A kick was skipped while "now < trackUntilMs" (a tracking tail). In a
    SLOW drag the cheap token repeats, so the 160 ms tail keeps expiring and
    the kick becomes eligible REPEATEDLY DURING the gesture -> item 2's
    "慢慢拖动时掉帧感变明显". Item 1's residual jank is the same walk
    bleeding into fast flings at tail boundaries.
  - Item 4's "白卡闪一下后消失": syncDense(wanted=0) hid the backing on ANY
    frame where denseTargets() returned empty (transient detach, 400 ms
    last-good-rect TTL expiry, or a discovery that had not run yet). Since
    OnPreDraw stops on a static page, no later scan could bring it back.
    The backing was also FallbackGlass.createView() = ~80% opaque white,
    which is precisely what the user perceived as a "白卡".

21.3 FIX A -- DELETE THE DETECTOR ENTIRELY (user's own suggestion)
  User: "现在显示出的玻璃卡可以根据上面玻璃卡的大小方式进行更改" -- i.e.
  stop detecting the paragraph; just reuse the glass card that is already
  correctly on screen. Implemented literally:
  - CardScanner.denseIndex(List<RectF>, float minHeightPx): PURE static
    function, O(rects.size()) with n=3..8, returns the LOWEST rect taller
    than the threshold. On the L3 help page that is the third container
    161,1556-1111,2385 (829 px); ordinary rows are 168..293 px.
  - RenderController.syncDense(rects, ...) borrows rects.get(idx) and calls
    the IDENTICAL MaterialBridge.configure() used for the host glass card,
    so backing and glass share insets, radius and per-frame alignment.
  - DELETED: Scanner.denseTargets(), oneOrEmpty(), discoverDense(),
    hasDenseParagraph(), findDenseParagraph(), denseParagraph WeakReference,
    lastDenseRect + 400 ms TTL, ID_ANDROID_SUMMARY / DENSE_SUMMARY_MIN_H /
    DISCOVERY_NODE_BUDGET; RenderController.denseKick Runnable,
    denseKickSync(), scheduleDenseKicks(), DENSE_KICK_DELAYS_MS,
    denseKickIndex, scheduleDenseKicks() call in refresh(), kick cancel in
    detach(). Frame path is now strictly scanAndBind() with zero tree walks,
    zero Handler timers and zero object allocations for P2a.

21.4 FIX B -- NO FLASH, NO LEAK, SOFTER COLOUR
  - denseLatchIndex retains the chosen glass card for the page's lifetime:
    one short rect can never hide the backing. Reset only by clearGlass()
    (real page / material change).
  - Feature is L3-ONLY (slot == ConfigContract.Slot.L3), so the latch can
    never leak a white block onto L1/L2. Verified geometry stays 829 px.
  - Backing now uses FallbackGlass.createLegibilityView() (~65% opaque,
    cool grey) instead of createView() (~80% white) -> reads as denser glass
    rather than an opaque white card.
  - New diagnostic format: "backing: idx=.. latch=.. rect=WxH attach=.. 
    enabled=.. slot=.. fallback=..". The old "dense backing: ... minH="
    and "attach=" strings no longer exist in the dex.

21.5 FIX C -- SLOW-DRAG SCHEDULING
  SCAN_INTERVAL_IDLE_MS 64 -> 16 ms. With a slow drag the token can repeat
  for several frames; once the tracking tail expired, the 64 ms gate produced
  a visible two-state stutter. 16 ms ~ one frame removes that step without
  going all the way back to the 0.3.2 "scan literally every frame" cost.
  A truly static page produces almost no pre-draw traversals anyway.

21.6 FIX D -- CHINESE, SIMPLIFIED CONFIG UI (SettingsActivity.java rewrite)
  Old: 40 flat English rows, each with an EditText + an "inherit from
  global" CheckBox; finding one switch meant a long scroll.
  New: all-Chinese titles/groups/labels/buttons/hints/toasts/diagnostics;
  a 9-item "常用设置" section expanded by default, four collapsible
  sections (背景与图片 / 玻璃高级参数 / 几何与描边 / 性能与调试) closed;
  true/false params are CheckBox switches instead of typed strings; slot
  spinner uses Chinese names (全局 / 一级菜单 / ... / 荣耀钱包); explicit
  dark background for legibility; header shows the build tag.
  Collapsing only changes visibility -- EVERY Row is still registered in
  rows[], so save() still writes all 40 params and inherit semantics are
  unchanged. valueOf()/setText() (used by the media picker shortcuts) were
  extended to handle the switch rows.

  0.3.5-p1 / code23, 57,769 B, v3-signed CN=MagicGlass,
  SHA256 dac4657217c1dd0d3cdd3d1e1936339c266d5b1af02ea25956bc730b3ef4343f.
  Gates: COMPILE_OK 136 class files (64 stubs, 14 sources), JAVALANG_OK 13,

22. 0.3.5-p2 -- BACKING SIZE FIX + BACKGROUND-EDIT CHAIN HARDENING
================================================================================

22.1 USER FEEDBACK (2026-11-12 13:31, verbatim)
  "1正常2已经是中文3慢滑和快滑都正常了，虽然还有优化空间，暂时可用了。
   4.L3个人热点下面帮助段落的半透明圆角衬底尺寸我看上去明显小，字都已经
   压在衬底外面了。5略微卡顿，可以使用。 继续进行开发吧 。"
  Decision: items 1/2/3/5 are ACCEPTED. The frame pipeline is now FROZEN --
  no scheduling, no pre-draw, no tracking-tail, no idle-gate changes in this
  round. Only item 4 and the background-edit backlog were touched.

22.2 ITEM 4 ROOT CAUSE: THE BACKING INHERITED THE DECORATIVE CARD INSET
  CardScanner.rects() deliberately shrinks every glass card by
  geo.hInsetDp = 14 dp and geo.vInsetDp = 4 dp so the host material does
  not touch the screen edge. Ordinary rows have their own internal padding,
  so that looks correct. The L3 help paragraph, however, is a PLAIN
  LinearLayout whose text runs to the un-inset edge.

  Device evidence (density 3.5, AAK-AN00):
    help block   LinearLayout     112,1542-1160,2399  = 1048 x 857
    glass rect   HnTransFrame     161,1556-1111,2385  =  950 x 829
    delta        left/right 49 px = 14 dp * 3.5
                 top/bottom 14 px =  4 dp * 3.5
  p1 borrowed the INSET rect verbatim -> the first ~3 glyphs of every help
  line sat OUTSIDE the backing. Live log from p1 (13:29:18):
    "backing: idx=2 latch=2 rect=950x829 attach=yes w=950 h=829 vis=0"
  So this was NOT a detection failure and NOT a sizing/attachment failure:
  the geometry source was simply the wrong (decorative) rectangle.

22.3 FIX (RenderController.syncDense / logDense)
  The backing still borrows the SAME card every frame and still uses the
  identical MaterialBridge.configure() call -- alignment, scroll tracking
  and the no-flicker denseLatchIndex are untouched. Only the target RectF
  changes:
    dx = geo.hInsetDp * density + 2 dp
    dy = geo.vInsetDp * density + 2 dp
    backing = (glass.left-dx, glass.top-dy, glass.right+dx, glass.bottom+dy)
  with a horizontal clamp to the overlay. On device this yields an
  approximately 1062 x 871 rect starting near 105,1535 -- fully covering
  the 1048 x 857 paragraph with a small anti-alias margin.
  Frame cost is unchanged: one O(rects.size()) loop + one RectF allocation
  + one configure(). NO tree walk, NO Handler, NO timer, NO new config key.
  Diagnostics upgraded: "card=WxH" (glass rect) plus
  "back=WxH@left,top" (actual backing), so the next test can verify the
  expansion numerically instead of by eye.
  Also corrected stale comments in ConfigContract.GLASS_DENSE_BACKING,
  CardScanner.denseIndex() and the RenderController header.

22.4 CONTINUED DEVELOPMENT -- BACKGROUND EDIT CHAIN (no frame-path edits)
  Audited the whole chain: SettingsActivity SAF picker -> ConfigStore
  .importMedia() (copies into filesDir/bg with path-traversal guard) ->
  ModuleProvider.openAssetFile() -> ConfigBus.media() -> BackdropLayer
  .decode() (two-pass sampled decode) -> ImageView. The chain is complete;
  three real defects were found and fixed:

  (a) TINT WAS ALWAYS INVISIBLE. applyTint() put tint AND dim on ONE View
      and finished with setAlpha(dim/100). With the default bg.dim=0, any
      user supplied #AARRGGBB tint was multiplied by zero. Replaced with a
      proper source-over composition of the two layers:
        aTint = Color.alpha(tintColor)/255, aDim = dim/100
        alpha = aTint + aDim*(1-aTint)
        rgb   = tintRGB * (aTint/alpha)   (black dim adds no RGB)
      The single View is kept, so overlay z-order is unchanged.
  (b) RANDOM BACKING NEVER ROTATED. bg.randomIntervalMin defaulted to 0 and
      apply() only schedules the timer when minutes > 0, so ticking
      "随机背景" merely re-rolled once per page entry. Default is now 5
      minutes; 0 still explicitly disables the timer. Added the Chinese
      hint "分钟；0 = 只在进页面时换，默认 5".
  (c) A failed decode left the previous full-size Bitmap attached to a GONE
      ImageView. showImage() now clears the drawable and recycles on failure.
  Toolchain: probe/compile_check.py's android.graphics.Color stub only had
  parseColor(); added alpha/red/green/blue/rgb/argb to mirror the real API
  (this was a stub gap, not a source defect).

22.5 BUILD, GATES, DEPLOYMENT

23. 0.3.6-p1 -- UNIFORM CARD TEMPLATE (REPLACE THE TWO-LAYER BACKING)
